1. EXECUTIVE SUMMARY
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

Maria adalah simulator RTL berbasis Rust (~157K LOC, 2619 test functions,
0 failing ✅). Arsitektur modern dengan arena allocator, SIMD lexer,
Cranelift JIT, cache manager, dan 12-region IEEE 1800 scheduler.


Namun untuk tape-out komersial SoC skala enterprise (9000+ modul,
100000+ file, 1M+ signal), Maria memiliki gap fundamental di hampir
setiap dimensi dibandingkan Verilator, VCS, Questa, dan Xcelium.

Enterprise Readiness Score:  67/100
Verification Readiness:      27/100
Simulator Score:             30/100
Compiler Score:              23/100
Simulator Score:             30/100
Compiler Score:              16/100
Scalability Score:           33/100

FINAL VERDICT: PROYEK RISET — BELUM SIAP PRODUKSI
Maria adalah proof-of-concept mengesankan untuk simulator HDL berbasis
Rust. Arsitektur modular dan IEEE 1800 compliance matrix 98% menunjukkan
pemahaman mendalam. Namun untuk tape-out komersial, dibutuhkan 5-10 tahun
pengembangan setara investasi puluhan juta dolar. Gap terbesar: tidak ada
formal verification, LSP/IDE, compiled-code simulation,
distributed/parallel simulation engine, coverage minimal,
constraint solver inadequate, waveform database tidak ada, CI/CD ✅ sudah ada,
tidak ada regression infrastructure, tidak ada VHDL/SystemC co-sim,
dan pre-existing test failures (ALL FIXED ✅ — 2032 tests pass, 0 fail,

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
2. ARCHITECTURE REVIEW
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

2.1 PIPE UTAMA
Preprocessor → Lexer → Parser → AST → Elaborator → IR → Engine → VCD/FST
Layer tambahan: HIR (lazy elab), MIR (opt), Cache, Diagnostics.
Arsitektur 3-tier (Frontend/AST/Backend) dengan cross-cutting layers.

MASALAH ARSITEKTUR KRITIS:

Arsitektur-01: ⚠️ Tidak ada SSA form — masih open
  Root Cause: IR (crates/maria-ir/src/ir.rs) adalah flat AST-style, bukan SSA.
  Impact: Tidak bisa optimasi global (CSE, DCE, LICM, GVN).
  Severity: Critical
  Fix: Implement SSA-based MIR dengan dominance frontier.
  Complexity: Phase 5-6 (6-12 bulan)

Arsitektur-02: ⚠️ Compiled-code simulation — masih open
  Root Cause: Simulator interpreter-based (evaluate_stmt_block
  men-traverse IR tree di runtime).
  Impact: 10-100x lebih lambat dari Verilator compiled C++.
  Severity: Critical
  Fix: Generate native code via Cranelift/LLVM untuk seluruh
  process body, bukan hanya expression eval.
  Complexity: Phase 7-8 (12-18 bulan)

Arsitektur-03: ⚠️ Engine single-threaded — masih open
  Root Cause: EventRegion Active/Inactive diproses dalam satu thread.
  Rayon hanya dipakai di DAG layer untuk Combinational processes.
  Impact: Amdahl's law — single-thread serial fraction ~80%.
  Severity: Critical
  Fix: Region-level parallelism, multi-threaded delta scheduler,
  lock-free signal state.
  Complexity: Phase 6-8 (12-18 bulan)

Arsitektur-04: ⚠️ Signal state Mutex — masih open
  Root Cause: state.signals adalah Vec<LogicVec> tanpa concurrent access.
  DAG parallel clone entire snapshot tiap cycle.
  Impact: Copy overhead O(N*M) untuk N signal M processes per cycle.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Row-based signal storage dengan fine-grained locking atau
  read-copy-update (RCU).
  Complexity: Phase 4-5

Arsitektur-05: ✅ Hierarchical timing wheel — FIXED
  Root Cause: events: Vec<Vec<RegionEvent>> dialokasikan apriori
  sebesar max_time+1. Drain/filter tiap delta siklus O(E).
  Impact: Memori O(max_time * events_per_time). Query overhead O(E)
  per delta cycle. Untuk sim 1M cycle, memori event queue bisa
  mencapai GB.
  Fix: Hierarchical timing wheel dengan bucket-based scheduling O(1)
  per event. Sub-offset remainder untuk cascade precision.
  Complexity: Phase 4-5
  STATUS: ✅ FIXED — 12 unit tests, 1160 total pass, 0 regression

Arsitektur-06: ⚠️ Elaborator monolithic — PARTIAL (ROUND 80)
  mod.rs turun dari 6012 → 5769 baris (-243). Ekstrak ke submodul:
  - classes.rs (270 baris): resolve_class_field_width, elaborate_classes,
    struct_override_fields
  - always.rs (310): always block elaboration
  - expr.rs (2328): expression elaboration
  - stmt.rs (2733): statement elaboration
  - flatten.rs (1108): hierarchy flattening
  - ext.rs (211): extensions
- sign.rs (BARU ROUND 128, ~200 baris): 4 helper signedness
  (elab_expr_is_signed, param_decl_is_signed, const_or_signed,
  expr_refs_signed_param) pindah verbatim; mod.rs 7059 → 6912 (-147).
- diag.rs (BARU ROUND 129, ~330 baris): 8 method diagnostic
  (elab_diag/at, elab_warn/at, find_name/literal_in_source,
  resolve_source_location, check_packed_width) + MAX_PACKED_WIDTH
  pindah verbatim (pub(crate)); call site tak berubah.
- topo.rs (BARU ROUND 130, ~190 baris): 4 fn instance-graph/top
  (collect_inst_names, build_inst_graph, module_cone_size,
  score_auto_top) pindah verbatim; 2 call site jadi topo::.
  ROUND 131: + compute_module_checksum + compute_topo_order (tanpa
  self → free fn) + header yatim dibersihkan.
- types.rs += enum helpers (ROUND 132): eval_enum_member_value +
  package_overlay (free) + verify_enum_members (method); 5 call site.
- timescale.rs (BARU ROUND 133, ~200 baris): 4 method delay-scale
  F84 (design_base_exp, delay_scale_for_unit,
  compute_module_delay_scales, current_delay_scale) pindah verbatim
  (pub(crate)); +doc yatim build_pkg_param_ctx dibersihkan.
- params.rs (BARU ROUND 134, ~560 baris): 3 method param resolution
  (resolve_param_values, build_pkg_param_ctx, collect_package_param_ctx)
  pindah verbatim (pub(crate)); mod.rs 6280 → 5754 (-526).
  Masih perlu: type resolution, param resolution, topo order, diagnostic
  helpers bisa dipisah lebih lanjut.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Lanjut ekstrak ke submodul (pass architecture).
  Complexity: Phase 3-4
  UPDATE ROUND 81: Fixed unclosed delimiter bug in mod.rs (2 impl Elaborator
  blocks without closing first). Added missing `}` before second impl block
  + fixed imports in types.rs (super::super::util::width, BUILTIN_UVM_CLASSES).
  cargo build --workspace + cargo test --workspace: 860 passed, 0 failed.

Arsitektur-07: ✅ Persistent design database — MICD (ROUND 77)
  MICD (Maria Incremental Compilation Database): object database biner
  di project/.maria/database/. Format MDB1 (header 64B + object table
  + payload), content-addressed via xxh3, dependency graph + transitive
  closure, verification cache, GC/eviction. Attach via
  CompileSession::attach_micd(), save via save_micd().
  Terintegrasi ke run/run_fast + maria-env DatabaseContext.
  Severity: High → RESOLVED

Arsitektur-08: ✅ Monorepo workspace (src/ → crates/) — FIXED (fondasi 11/12)
  Root Cause: Seluruh logika (~157K LOC, 28 modul) hidup dalam SATU crate
  monolitik `maria`. Semua modul saling terlihat tanpa batas dependensi
  eksplisit; perubahan satu file memicu rebuild modul besar; test tidak
  terisolasi per komponen; grafik dependensi tidak terdokumentasi.
  Impact: Maintainability rendah, compile unit besar (borrow-check seluruh
  project), dependency graph implisit, sulit diuji terpisah.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Workspace Cargo dengan crate bottom-up acyclic. Rencana 12 crate:
    1.  maria-core        ← intern, arena, error, diagnostics, config, animasi, logic
    2.  maria-ast         ← ast (expr, stmt, types, const_eval, inline)
    3.  maria-ir          ← ir (IrDesign/IrStmt/IrExpr; re-export LogicVec dari core)
    4.  maria-parser      ← parser                    (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    5.  maria-elaboration ← elaboration               (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    6.  maria-compiler    ← frontend+cache+micd+hir+mir+profiling (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    7.  maria-simulator   ← simulator+waveform+scheduler+debugger+vpi (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    8.  maria-mv          ← mv (Maria HDL)            (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    9.  maria-formal      ← formal                    (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    10. maria-env         ← env + lsp                 (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    11. maria-tools       ← tools                     (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    12. maria-gui         ← gui (egui)                (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    13. maria-api         ← src/lib.rs (API publik + re-export maria_*) (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
    14. maria-tests       ← src/tests + edge_tests + debug_lexer (SELESAI — 2026-08-10)
  Complexity: Phase bertahap — 14/14 member, target akhir tercapai: src/ = main.rs + cli.rs
  STATUS: ✅ FIXED (selesai) — 2026-08-10

    priority} (dipakai compiler/env; sim_dag/clock_domain/cdc tetap di
    src/scheduler utk crate 7 — sim_dag bergantung pada crate::simulator::parallel,
    memutusnya akan membuat cycle compiler→simulator) pindah ke
    crates/maria-compiler (~17K LOC). Self-ref micd/ast.rs test
    `crate::SessionConfig` → `crate::frontend::compile_session::SessionConfig`;
    konsumen (env cache/database/telemetry/runtime/compiler, gui/backend,
    lsp, tools 5 file, tests 4 file, simulator engine/uvm, lib.rs, main.rs)
    ganti `crate::frontend|micd|cache|hir|mir|profiling` → `maria_compiler::*`
    dan `maria::scheduler::*` cluster pindahan → `maria_compiler::scheduler::*`
    (src/scheduler/mod.rs re-export agar path polos tetap valid).
    Cargo.toml: +num_cpus, parking_lot, serde_json, walkdir. Test path
    relatif `test/counter.sv` (cwd test = direktori crate) → helper
    `root_rel()` berbasis CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR.
  UPDATE 5/12 (maria-elaboration): seluruh src/elaboration/ (elaborator/
    always,expr,ext,flatten,mod,stmt + util/ 9 submodule + lib.rs ex mod.rs)
    pindah ke crates/maria-elaboration. Self-ref `crate::elaboration::util::*`
    → `crate::util::*`; dependensi ke `crate::cache::checksum` diputus dengan
    memindahkan checksum.rs ke maria-core (pola LogicVal) — cache re-export
    `pub use maria_core::checksum` agar micd dkk tetap valid. Konsumen (env,
    frontend, lsp, tools, tests, simulator/eval/ast, lib.rs, main.rs) ganti
    `crate::elaboration`/`maria::elaboration` → `maria_elaboration`.
  UPDATE 4/12 (maria-parser): seluruh src/parser/ (lexer, preprocessor,
    util, specify, udp, config, package, class, expr, stmt, decl, instance,
    proc + Parser di mod.rs → lib.rs) pindah ke crates/maria-parser (~11.3K
    LOC, 13 file). Self-ref `crate::parser::lexer::*` → `crate::lexer::*`;
    `maria_ir::LogicVal` (FillLit token) → `maria_core::LogicVal` (parser
    kini tidak lagi depend ke maria-ir); debug print `crate::tools::expr_to_string`
    → `format!("{:?}", cond)` (hindari dependensi parser→tools). Konsumen
    (frontend, env/compiler, lsp, tools/fmt, tests, main.rs, lib.rs) ganti
    `crate::parser`/`maria::parser` → `maria_parser`. `env/compiler/parser.rs`
    (wrapper parse/parse_strict) tetap di src/ — bukan bagian parser crate.
    Target akhir (keputusan user): src/ hanya main.rs + cli.rs — seluruh
    logika pindah ke crates, sesi lanjutan membawa crate 5-12.
  Cycle breaker: LogicVal/LogicVec dipindah dari src/ir/ir.rs ke
    maria-core/src/logic.rs (memutus siklus ast↔ir: ast::Expr::FillLit
    memakai LogicVal). maria-ir re-export maria_core::{LogicVal, LogicVec}
    agar `maria_ir::LogicVec` dan `use maria_ir::*` tetap valid.
  Visibility: DataType::width()/DeclKind::default_width() di maria-ast
    diubah pub(crate)→pub (dipakai elaboration lintas crate).
  Orphan rule: impl Display for LogicVal/LogicVec pindah ke maria-core
    (Rust orphan rules melarang impl trait untuk tipe asing).
  API root: lib.rs tetap re-export maria_core::{Symbol, Span, SimError,
    DiagCode, ...} — `maria::compile_str`/`simulate_str` dkk TIDAK berubah.
  Build: `cargo build --workspace`, `cargo test --workspace`.

2.2 SCHEDULER ARCHITECTURE

Scheduler mengklaim 12-region IEEE 1800 compliance, dan implementasi
di core.rs menunjukkan ke-12 region diproses dalam loop. Namun:

Sched-01: ✅ Postponed region — FIXED (ditambahkan ke IEEE_REGIONS)
   Root Cause: $strobe dan $monitor dipanggil SETELAH delta loop.
   Impact: IEEE 1800-2017 Section 4.5.4 violation.
   Severity: High
   Fix: EventRegion::Postponed ditambahkan, diproses 1x per time step.

Sched-02: ✅ NBA commit — FIXED (di Nba region handler)
   Root Cause: Sebelumnya commit_nba() hanya di Reactive branch.
   Impact: NBA region di-loop sebelum Reactive → commit 1 region delay.
   Severity: High
   Fix: Commit NBA dipindahkan ke EventRegion::Nba region handler.
   STATUS: ✅ FIXED — self.commit_nba() di EventRegion::Nba (core.rs:1344)

Sched-03: ✅ Delta limit warning — FIXED
   Root Cause: if delta_count > 10_000_000 { return Err }
   Impact: Debugging zero-delay loops impossible.
   Severity: Medium
   Fix: Warning setiap 100K delta, configurable max 20M.

Sched-04: ✅ Preponed snapshot per delta — FIXED
   Root Cause: Snapshot di-update per cycle bukan per delta.
   Impact: Edge detection pakai snapshot stale di multi-delta.
   Severity: Critical
   Fix: signal_snapshot di-refresh setiap delta cycle.

2.3 MEMORY ARCHITECTURE

Mem-01: ✅ Signal history bounded + disk spill (ROUND 79)
  SignalHistoryStore (signal_history.rs): ring buffer per-signal
  memory_per_signal (default 10K entries), disk spill via
  BufWriter append-only (enable_spill()). stats: total_spilled,
  total_memory. Offload lama ke disk → memory bounded.
  state.signals tetap Vec<LogicVec> (fixed-width, kompak).
  Severity: Critical → RESOLVED (history), state tetap in-memory
  (by design — simulation requires random-access).

Mem-02: ✅ Memory budget — CacheManager LRU eviction (ROUND 77)
  CacheStore<V> (cache_manager.rs) punya budget: AtomicU64,
  insert() check budget → evict_lru(needed) → collect entries
  by access time (oldest first), remove sampai target tercapai.
  CacheManager::with_budgets(ast=64MB, hir=64MB, include=32MB,
  macro=16MB). hit_rate() + memory_used() untuk monitoring.
  Severity: High → RESOLVED

Mem-03: ✅ mmap semua files — FIXED (ROUND 88)
           MmapFile (frontend/io.rs) versi lama MENAHAN region Mmap di
           struct walau isinya sudah di-copy ke heap `bytes` (field
           dead_code) → memori dobel + address space tertahan per file
           tanpa batas jumlah file concurrent. Fix: mapping dilepas
           SEGERA setelah salinan dibuat (mmap-copy path untuk file
           >=4KB; file kecil read biasa) — tidak ada map tertahan selama
           compile, address space langsung bebas. Zero-copy penuh tetap
           di jalur MICD (micd/format.rs) yang memang membaca database
           ter-map dengan scope terbatas. Severity: Medium → RESOLVED
  Root Cause: FastLexer + MmapFile mmap seluruh file sebelum
  diproses. Tidak ada limit jumlah file concurrent.
  Impact: 100K files x 10KB average = 1GB virtual address.
  File >4GB bisa exhaust address space di 32-bit.
  Severity: Medium
  Fix: Stream processing dengan read-ahead buffer, mmap
  windowing.

═══ 2.4 JIT ARCHITECTURE ═══

JIT-01: ⚠️ JIT hanya expression — masih open
  Root Cause: CompiledExpr hanya compile binary/unary/const ops.
  Evaluate_stmt_block tetap interpreted.
  Impact: Speedup minimal di simulation (Amdahl: bottleneck di
  interpreter).
  Severity: Critical
  Fix: Compile seluruh process body ke native code. Generate
  state transitions natively.

JIT-02: ⚠️ JIT tidak cache di disk — masih open
  Root Cause: JITCache in-memory only. Cold sim selalu
  recompile.
  Impact: Startup lambat, terutama untuk large designs.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Persistent JIT cache dengan content-addressable storage.

═══ 2.5 PARALLEL ARCHITECTURE ═══

Par-01: ⚠️ Work-stealing scheduler — skeleton ada, tidak terintegrasi (ROUND 77)
  Scheduler/work_stealing.rs: crossbeam-segqueue + Worker + 12 test
  lulus, tapi semua execute_task() branch no-op. Tidak terhubung ke
  CompileSession atau SimulationEngine. Butuh implementasi task
  handlers + integrasi pipeline.
  Impact: CPU utilization rendah saat sim.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Implement task handlers + integrasi ke CompileSession::compile().

Par-02: ⚠️ DAG hanya combinational — masih open
  Root Cause: Sequential processes (AlwaysFF, AlwyasWithDelay)
  bypass DAG dan jalan sequential.
  Impact: Sebagian besar processes di desain sinkron —
  paralelisme rendah.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Clock-domain-based parallel scheduling untuk sequential
  processes.

Par-03: ⚠️ Clone bodies di parallel eval — masih open
  Root Cause: Setiap DAG layer, seluruh body proses di-clone.
  Signal snapshot di-clone.
  Impact: Memory thrashing untuk body besar. Allocator pressure.
  Severity: High
  Fix: Arc-based process body sharing, differential snapshot.

4. GAP ANALYSIS — DETAILED BY CATEGORY
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

4.1 LANGUAGE DESIGN GAPS

LANG-01: [] Tidak ada VHDL Support (Critical)
LANG-02: [] Tidak ada SystemC Support (Critical)
LANG-03: ✅ PSL boolean (IEEE 1850) (ROUND 74) — `assert always (expr)
         @(posedge clk);` / `assert never (expr) @(posedge clk);` di-parse
         → Stmt::Assert ber-clock (always = properti true tiap cycle;
         never = cond dibalik !). Reuse jalur assertion module-level
         (PropertyAssert → always block ber-clock, ROUND 71) → evaluasi
         tiap posedge + assertion_stats. Directive `default clock =
         posedge clk;` di-parse & dibuang (arm Token::Default di module
         body — tanpa efek runtime). Operator temporal PSL (`|->`,
         `until`, `before`, `next`) tidak dikenal lexer → parse gagal →
         rollback + skip assertion (modul utuh, perilaku sama temporal SVA
         LANG-04). 2 test (boolean pass + temporal skipped safe).
LANG-04: ✅ `assert property` module-level boolean + temporal ##N
         (ROUND 71/75) — bentuk `assert property (@(posedge clk) expr)`
         di-parse → ModuleItem::PropertyAssert → elaborator wrap always
         block ber-clock. Operator temporal `##N` (delay k cycle) kini
         bekerja penuh: parser lex `##` → `Token::HashHash`, parse delay
         `N`/`[min:max]`, hasilkan `Sequence::Concat(Delay, Expr)`;
         elaborator → `IrStmt::Assert { sequence: Some(...) }`;
         engine push `SequenceAttempt` setiap posedge, evaluator
         `eval_sequence_depth` rekursif (Expr/Delay/Concat/Or/And/Repeat)
         dengan depth-aware historical snapshot. Fix critical: extra arm
         `IrStmt::Assert` di `evaluate_stmt_block` (sebelumnya sequence
         di-ignore → assertion immediate → 0 sequence match). 2 test
         (pass `a##1 b` + fail `a##1 b` tanpa b). `|->` overlap
         implication (IEEE §1800-2017 §16.9.2) kini didukung: lexer
         `Token::PipeArrow` (`|->`), AST `Sequence::Implication(ante, cons)`,
         IR `IrSequence::Implication`, evaluator stateful via `ante_matched`
         field di `SequenceAttempt` + vacuous truth (ante tidak pernah
         match → pass). Test vacuous (req=0 → 0 fail).
         `##[min:max]` range delay (IEEE §1800-2017 §16.9.2.2): parse
         `[min:max]` → `DelayRange(min, max)`, evaluator iterasi k dalam
         range → match jika b true di cycle k ∈ [min,max]. 3 test:
         pass min match, pass max match, fail (b never true). `[*]` tetap open.
LANG-05: [x] Constraint solver — RE-VERIFIKASI (ROUND 59): domain analysis +
         guided generation + bounded backtracking 10K (bukan rejection 100) —
         uvm/constraint_solver.rs solve_constraints (domain equality/inequality/
         inside/dist + guided generation) + CRIT-002. Marker audit stale.
LANG-06: ✅ IEEE 1800-2017 feature coverage report (ROUND 94)
         crates/maria-tools/src/ieee_coverage.rs: CoverageReport.
         65+ features: section/status/notes. Stats: full/partial/unsup.
         Text report + JSON serializable. Coverage ~70% full/partial.
         5 test: generation, stats, text, all_statuses, serialize.
LANG-07: [x] `ifdef expression syntax — RE-VERIFIKASI (ROUND 59): preprocessor
         eval_ifdef_expr (|| && ! ( ) defined, `elsif) — CRIT-046. Marker stale.
LANG-08: ✅ net alias / nettype (ROUND 70) — `alias a = b = c;`
           (IEEE 1800-2017 §10.9): semua net dalam rantai jadi satu jaringan
           (short) — lexer Token::Alias + Token::Nettype, AST
           ModuleItem::NetAlias(Vec<(Expr,Expr)>) + NettypeDecl(base, range),
           parser `alias`/`nettype` (dgn range `[msb:lsb]` + klausa `with`),
           elaborator resolve nama → SignalId → union-find → canonical →
           IrDesign.net_aliases: HashMap<SignalId,SignalId>; engine
           SimulationState.alias_redirect mer-direct read_signal/write_signal
           member ke canonical (tulis salah satu → semua terlihat).
           `nettype logic [7:0] mynet;` — tipe net user-defined: nama
           didaftarkan ke module_type_params (parser) + typedef_map/typedef_dims
           (elaborator, lebar = base × range) sehingga `mynet x;` ter-resolve
           seperti typedef. 3 test (alias short, alias chain 3-net, nettype
           8-bit 0xAB).
LANG-09: [x] let declarations — RE-VERIFIKASI (ROUND 59): SAMA dengan LANG-40
         (let declaration FIXED 2026-08-14 — Token::Let, LetDecl, let_decls,
         resolve via elaborate_expr, substitute_let_args, 2 test). Marker stale.
LANG-10: ✅ checker construct (SVA checker) (ROUND 72) — `checker name
           (ports); assert/cover property...; endchecker` (IEEE 1800-2017
           §17.8): lexer Token::Checker/EndChecker; parser parse deklarasi
           (port positional/named + body items sampai endchecker) →
           ModuleItem::Checker(CheckerDecl{name, ports, items}); elaborator

LANG-11: ✅ restrict property (ROUND 71) — Token::Restrict (lexer) +

LANG-12: ✅ assume property module-level (ROUND 71) — `assume property

LANG-13: ✅ cover property module-level (ROUND 71) — `cover property

WAV-16: ✅ Protocol-aware waveform decode — mwave `decode` (2026-08-24):
           `maria mwave decode <vcd> --proto apb|axi4lite|ahb` — dekode
           transaksi bus dari timeline VCD (wave.rs). APB: SETUP
           (psel&!penable, latch addr/wdata/dir) → ACCESS selesai saat
           pready=1 (pready absen = selalu ready); wait-state didukung.
           AXI4-Lite: handshake valid/ready per channel (aw+w→b untuk write,
           ar→r untuk read, resp bresp/rresp tercatat), transaksi merge-sort
           by end-time. AHB-Lite: address phase NONSEQ/SEQ dgn hready=1,
           data phase selesai di hready=1 berikutnya (pipelining back-to-back
           ditangani). Sinyal dicari by nama komponen terakhir (case-
           insensitive fallback) sehingga scope nested tetap ketemu. Output:
           daftar transaksi (index/kind/start/end/addr/data/resp) + summary
           write/read. 5 test unit inline wave.rs (apb write+read+wait-state,
           axi4lite 2 channel + resp, ahb single transfer, error sinyal
           hilang, helper sample_at/bits_val) + demo e2e .sv → VCD → decode:
           apb_demo.sv (4 tx benar), axi_demo.sv (3 tx), ahb_demo.sv (2 tx).

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
6. TOP 200 PERFORMANCE BOTTLENECKS
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

(Top 30 — full 200 di appendix)

PERF-01: ✅ Hierarchical timing wheel — O(1) event scheduling
         core.rs: self.events[t].retain() + iter()
         Impact: Setiap delta cycle scan seluruh event list
         Fix: Region-specific event bins with O(1) access

PERF-02: ✅ Signal history — FIXED (stale; ROUND 9 + CRIT-019)
         core.rs: signal_history per signal → kini SignalHistoryStore
         (signal_history.rs) ring buffer per-signal (default 10K) +
         disk spill opsional (Mem-01). Marker audit tidak diupdate
         saat ROUND 9.

PERF-03:[] Preponed snapshot clones ALL signals every cycle
         core.rs: snapshot Vec<LogicVec> per cycle
         Impact: O(S * W) copy per cycle (S=signals, W=avg width)
         Fix: Lazy/differential snapshot, version-based

PERF-04:[] Process body clone in DAG parallel evaluation
         core.rs: body_map clone for each DAG execution
         Impact: O(P * B) memory/copy per cycle
         Fix: Arc<[IrStmt]> sharing

PERF-05:[] Vec::drain/filter/retain in event loop — O(E) allocation
         core.rs: drain/filter/retain per region per delta
         Impact: Each delta cycle allocates temporary Vecs
         Fix: Linked-list of events per region, O(1) pop

PERF-06:[] Signal state Vec behind &mut self — no concurrent reads
         state.rs: read_signal/write_signal sequential
         Impact: All process evaluation serialized on state access
         Fix: Row-based signal storage, versioned reads

PERF-07: ✅ LogicVec SIMD-ized with u64 bitmasks
         src/simulator/value.rs:
         - to_bitmasks/from_bitmasks: u64 (known, value) encodings
         - has_xz: O(1) via known-mask check (was O(width) per call)
         - bitwise_op: u64 fast path with zero-extension for ≤64 bits
         - RedAnd/RedOr/RedXor: u64 AND/OR/count_ones (O(1))
         - Shl/Shr/Sshl/Sshr: direct u64 shifts (O(1))
         - BitNot: u64 bitwise NOT on bitmasks (O(1))
         - Minus: u64 two's complement (!v + 1) (O(1))
         - Eq/Neq: u64 bitmask comparison lk==rk && lv==rv (O(1))
         Fallback ke per-bit loop untuk width > 64.
         STATUS: ✅ FIXED — O(width)→O(1) untuk ≤64 bit widths

PERF-08: ✅ StringTable thread-local cache (ROUND 91) — crates/maria-core/
         intern/string_intern.rs: TL_CACHE thread_local HashMap<String,
         Symbol> (max 256 entries). Intern check thread-local dulu → DashMap.
         Eviction: clear half saat penuh. Mengurangi DashMap contention pada
         thread concurrent.

PERF-09: [x] CacheManager LRU eviction — FIXED (ROUND 77, Mem-02)
         CacheStore<V> punya budget: AtomicU64, insert() check budget
         → evict_lru(needed). CacheManager::with_budgets(ast=64MB, hir=64MB,
         include=32MB, macro=16MB). hit_rate() + memory_used().

PERF-10: ✅ Recursive evaluate_expr depth protection — FIXED (stale; ROUND 4)
         eval/expr.rs: expr_recursion_depth > 4096 → error terstruktur
         (bukan stack overflow diam-diam). Marker audit tidak diupdate
         sejak fix.

PERF-11:[x] $display string formatting allocates per call
         eval.rs: format_display creates String each time
         Impact: Heavy $display usage destroys performance
         Fix: Write to io::Write directly with format segments
         FIX (ROUND 27): borrow fmt_str (as_str, no clone per call), arg
              evaluasi lazy (no Vec per call), String::with_capacity, write!
              langsung untuk %d/%h/%f/%b (no String perantara), helper
              u64_digits/u64_hex_digits untuk padding.

PERF-12: ✅ File-based JIT cache persistence (line-based format)
         src/simulator/jit_cranelift.rs: cache_path + save_cache() / load_cache()
         Format: line-based "hash|name|arg_count|width" per entry (no serde dep)
         Auto-save via cache_insert(), auto-load via set_cache_path()
         Placeholder entries with null code_ptr detected (return None = miss → re-JIT)
         STATUS: ✅ FIXED — persistent JIT cache metadata across runs

PERF-13: [x] VCD background writer — FIXED (ROUND 86, WAV-19)
         VcdWriter Out enum { File | Bg | Detached } + BgWriter (mpsc
         channel + writer thread). enable_background() opt-in. CLI:
         --waveform-bg. E2e demo.sv --waveform-bg IDENTIK sinkron.

PERF-14: ✅ VCD streaming — FIXED via WAV-06 (periodic flush +
           stream_flush_interval). Marker audit stale — streaming sudah ada.
         core.rs: dump called per cycle tapi buffered
         Impact: Large memory for signal history before dump
         Fix: Stream VCD to disk continuously

PERF-15: ✅ NBA commit — O(1) conflict detection via signal map (ROUND 90)
           crates/maria-simulator/src/simulator/engine/scheduler/event.rs: nba_signal_map
           HashMap<SignalId, usize> tracks which signals have pending NBA writes.
           push_nba_pending: O(1) conflict detection (HashMap::contains_key replaces
           linear scan of nba_pending). commit_nba: clear map after drain.
           Dampak: conflict detection naik dari O(N) ke O(1) per push.

PERF-16: ✅ Covergroup sampling — constant-bin hash lookup (ROUND 90)
           crates/maria-simulator/src/simulator/engine/coverage.rs: build_const_bin_map
           pre-computes HashMap<u64, (Symbol, BinType)> untuk bins dengan constant
           ranges (IrExpr::Const). Lazy cache di covergroup_const_bins field.
           O(1) lookup pertama → fallback linear scan hanya untuk transition bins +
           expression ranges. Non-const bins (transitions + expr) di-scan linear
           hanya bila const map miss. Dampak: covergroup dengan bins konstan
           (kasus umum: enum/encode values) naik dari O(B) ke O(1) per sample.

PERF-17: ✅ All file mmaps held open during compile — FIXED (ROUND 88)
         io.rs: MmapFile tidak lagi menahan region map — mapping
         dilepas segera setelah isi disalin ke heap (lihat Mem-03).
         100K file compile tidak lagi memegang 100K mapping.

PERF-18: [x] StringTable.get() — FIXED (already O(1) &str)
         StringTable::get() mengembalikan &str langsung dari intern
         storage tanpa alokasi String baru. Marker audit stale.

PERF-19: ✅ Parser recovery retries same tokens — STALE TERVERIFIKASI
         (ROUND 86, commit 237c358): recovery kini selalu skip-to-sync-
         token (`skip_until_semi_or_end`/`skip_to_stmt_boundary`) + guard
         kemajuan (`if pos == before { advance() }`, komentar flood 71 rb
         E1002 di stmt.rs) — persis fix yang disarankan audit. Bukti
         empirik: 9 kasus malformed → 0 duplikat (line,col), maks 2
         error/kasus, posisi akurat; 500 stmt rusak → ≤500 error (1/stmt).
         Test regresi: parser_recovery_tests.rs (4 test, bound min/max +
         anti-duplikat + akurasi posisi + source sehat tetap bersih).

PERF-20: ✅ ForkJoin tracking Vec iteration — STALE TERVERIFIKASI
         (ROUND 87, commit bc7a008): `fork_decrement(fid)` = indeks
         langsung `fork_groups[fid]` O(1) (fid dari Continuation);
         alloc O(1) via free-list `fork_free`. Scan `fork_groups` hanya
         per-statement `wait fork`/`disable fork`/`disable <label>` —
         bukan per-completion. Scheduler single-threaded → "atomic
         counter" tak relevan (`remaining: usize` sudah counter).
         Test regresi: fork_tracking_tests.rs (4 test — slot reuse
         fired-reset, fanout 64 branch non-premature t=63, isolasi
         group nested, filter spawner wait fork).

         DPI-C FFI (24), mmap (2), isatty (2), engine call (1),
         from_utf8_unchecked (4 — sudah di-fix sebelumnya).
         Kesimpulan: Semua unsafe inherent, tidak bisa direfactor.
         Safety: well-encapsulated dengan proper invariants.
         Severity: Medium — STATUS: ✅ FIXED (audit completed)
         Impact: unsafe blocks unchecked for soundness
         Severity: High

DEBT-17:[x] Simulation state not Send/Sync audited
         Impact: Thread safety assumptions unverified
         Severity: High
         FIX: Compile-time guard test_simulation_state_send_sync_audit
              (tests/mod.rs) — assert Send+Sync untuk SimulationState
              (murni Vec/HashMap/u64/TimeFormat) dan LogicVec. Engine
              sengaja tidak di-assert: Option<FstWaveWriter> (wavefst)
              memegang RefCell+raw pointer (symbol cache FST) → !Sync
              by design; parallel.rs hanya clone LogicVec via rayon.

DEBT-18:[x] Symbol interning relies on 'static lifetime
         string_intern.rs: leaked allocation
         Impact: Memory never freed — okay for compiler, bad
         for daemon
         Severity: Medium
         FIX: reset_string_table() (string_intern.rs:259) — dibebaskan
              untuk daemon mode (detail entri fix di bawah).

DEBT-19:[x] No Drop implementation for shared resources
         Impact: File handles may leak on early return
         Severity: Medium
         FIX: impl Drop VcdWriter→flush() (pola CsvWaveWriter) + impl Drop
              FstWaveWriter→close() (FstWriter::finish() via writer.take(),
              idempoten) — file ter-flush/finalisasi walau engine return
              early tanpa close() eksplisit (detail di ROUND 26).

DEBT-20:[x] No Debug/Deref implementations consistent across types
         Impact: Debugging state difficult
         Severity: Low
         FIX: Debug ringkas konsisten untuk VcdWriter (manual: enabled,
              max_dump_size, total_written, signal_count, code_count) dan
              FstWaveWriter (manual: writer_active, var_count, current_time)
              — FstWriter wavefst tidak Debug, jadi manual; SignalStats
              #[derive(Debug)]. CsvWaveWriter sudah Debug (detail ROUND 26).

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
9. BENCHMARK PREDICTION
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

9.1 COMPILE TIME (Single module, small design)

| Simulator       | Cold Compile | Warm Compile | Incremental |
|-----------------|-------------|--------------|-------------|
| Verilator 5.x   | ~2.5s       | ~0.3s        | ~0.2s       |
| VCS             | ~3.0s       | ~1.0s        | ~0.5s       |
| Questa          | ~1.5s       | ~0.5s        | ~0.3s       |
| Xcelium         | ~2.0s       | ~0.8s        | ~0.4s       |
| Riviera-PRO     | ~0.8s       | ~0.3s        | ~0.2s       |
| ModelSim        | ~0.5s       | ~0.2s        | ~0.15s      |
| Maria (--fast)  | ~0.08s      | ~0.04s       | ~0.02s      |

Analisis: Maria lebih cepat untuk compile karena tidak melakukan
optimasi, code generation, atau elaborate analysis yang dilakukan
Verilator/VCS. Ini BUKAN keunggulan — ini trade-off: Maria compile
cepat karena hanya interpret AST/IR, sementara Verilator spends
waktu untuk generate optimized C++.

9.2 SIMULATION TIME (Large SoC, ~1M cycles)

| Simulator       | RTL Sim    | Gate Sim  | Relative to Maria |
|-----------------|-----------|-----------|-------------------|
| Verilator 5.x   | ~0.5s     | N/A       | ~0.01x            |
| VCS             | ~1.0s     | ~5.0s     | ~0.02x            |
| Questa          | ~2.0s     | ~8.0s     | ~0.04x            |
| Xcelium         | ~1.5s     | ~6.0s     | ~0.03x            |
| Riviera-PRO     | ~3.0s     | ~10.0s    | ~0.06x            |
| ModelSim        | ~5.0s     | ~15.0s    | ~0.1x             |
| Maria (est)     | ~50-100s  | N/A       | 1x (baseline)     |

Analisis: Maria interpreter-based diperkirakan 50-100x lebih lambat
dari Verilator. Untuk SoC 10M cycle = Maria 30+ menit. Untuk
industrial regression dengan 10K tests = Maria 200+ hari vs
Verilator 2 hari.

9.3 MEMORY USAGE (10K module design)

| Simulator       | Elab Mem  | Sim Mem   | History       |
|-----------------|-----------|-----------|---------------|
| Verilator       | ~500MB    | ~200MB    | N/A (compile) |
| VCS             | ~1.5GB    | ~800MB    | Dynamic       |
| Questa          | ~1.2GB    | ~600MB    | Dynamic       |
| Maria (est)     | ~3-5GB    | ~2-3GB    | Unlimited     |

Analisis: Maria menyimpan full IR in memory + signal history.
Tanpa database backend dan tanpa compiled code, memori Maria
untuk large designs diperkirakan 3-5x lebih tinggi.

9.4 PARALLEL SCALING (16-core AMD EPYC)

| Simulator       | Speedup 16c | Efficiency |
|-----------------|-------------|------------|
| Verilator       | 8-12x       | 50-75%     |
| VCS             | 10-14x      | 62-87%     |
| Questa          | 6-10x       | 37-62%     |
| Xcelium         | 8-12x       | 50-75%     |
| Maria (est)     | 1.5-2x      | 9-12%      |

Analisis: Maria saat ini single-threaded untuk sebagian besar
simulasi. Rayon DAG parallel hanya mencakup Combinational processes.
Amdahl's law: dengan 80% serial, max speedup = 1/(0.8+0.2/16) =
~1.2x.

9.5 STARTUP TIME (Cold + Warm)

| Simulator       | Startup (no sim) | With sim 1M cycles |
|-----------------|------------------|-------------------|
| Verilator       | ~2.5s (compile)  | ~3.0s             |
| VCS             | ~3.0s            | ~4.0s             |
| Questa          | ~1.5s            | ~3.5s             |
| Maria           | ~0.1s            | ~50-100s          |

9.6 WAVEFORM SPEED (1M signals, 1M cycles)

| Simulator       | Dump Time  | File Size | Load Time   |
|-----------------|------------|-----------|-------------|
| VCS+VPD+Verdi  | ~30s        | ~2GB      | ~2s         |
| Questa+WDB     | ~45s        | ~1.5GB    | ~3s         |
| Verilator+FST  | ~60s        | ~1GB      | ~5s         |
| Maria+FST      | ~600s (est) | ~3GB      | N/A (no viewer) |

Analisis: Maria tanpa FSDB/VPD dan dengan VCD writer yang blocking,
waveform dump untuk large design diperkirakan lambat 10-20x dari
commercial tools.

18. FINAL VERDICT

PROYEK RISET — BELUM SIAP TAPE-OUT KOMERSIAL
============================================

Maria adalah proof-of-concept arsitektur simulator HDL modern
dalam Rust yang menunjukkan pemahaman mendalam tentang IEEE 1800
dan teknik compiler modern. Namun untuk digunakan dalam tape-out
chip komersial (ASIC/SoC skala enterprise), terdapat gap
fundamental yang membutuhkan investasi 5-10 tahun dan puluhan
juta dolar untuk mencapai paritas dengan Verilator/VCS/Questa.

ARGUMEN TEKNIS RINCI:

1. SIMULATION PERFORMANCE GAP
   Interpreter-based design → 50-100x lebih lambat dari Verilator
   compiled-C++ dan 20-50x lebih lambat dari VCS native.
   Untuk regression 10K tests x 1M cycles = tidak feasible.

2. COMPILER OPTIMIZATION ABSENCE
   Tidak ada SSA, CSE, DCE, LICM, atau optimization pass apapun.
   MIR opt hanya 142 lines. Ini membuat Maria setara compiler
   undergraduate project, bukan production EDA tool.

3. VERIFICATION INFRASTRUCTURE GAP KRITIS
   Tidak ada code coverage = tidak bisa sign-off tape-out.
   Tidak ada UCDB/UCM coverage database = coverage tidak bisa
   dianalisis multi-run. Constraint solver rejection sampling =
   tidak bisa handle complex constraints.

4. LANGUAGE SUPPORT GAP FATAL
   Dynamic arrays, associative arrays, queues — semua tidak
   support. Ini blocking untuk testbench development.
   uvm_callback, uvm_reg, uvm_tlm — tidak ada. Semua UVM VIP
   komersial depend on these.

5. ENTERPRISE INFRASTRUCTURE KOSONG
   Tidak ada CI/CD, regression management, distributed build,
   license management, IP protection, API, scripting, atau
   tool integration. This is not deployable in any
   professional engineering team.

6. EDA ECOSYSTEM ISOLATION
   No VPI/PLI = tidak bisa integrasi dengan Verdi, SpyGlass,
   JasperGold, PrimeTime, atau EDA tool apapun.
   No FSDB = waveform tidak bisa dibuka di Verdi.
   No UCDB = coverage tidak bisa di-merge.

7. TESTING INFRASTRUCTURE RAPUH
   6 pre-existing test failures = confidence rendah.
   Stress tests ignored = large design paths untested.
   No sanitizer tests = memory safety unverified.

8. IDE/LSP ABSOLUTELY ABSENT
   Developer productivity untuk SV engineers sangat bergantung
   pada LSP. Tanpa LSP, Maria tidak bisa diadopsi di tim
   engineering profesional.

CORE STRENGTHS (pengakuan):

+ Arsitektur modular bersih dengan cross-cutting layers
+ Incremental compile sangat cepat (~0.45s untuk 10K modules)
+ SIMD lexer menunjukkan pemikiran performa modern
+ Zero-deallocation sim arena — teknik memory management baik
+ IEEE 1800 12-region scheduler — implementasi paling correct
  di open source
+ Cranelift JIT integration — langkah tepat meski masih early
+ String interning O(1) dengan DashMap — scalable
+ Testing cukup comprehensive (951 test functions)
+ Dokumentasi arsitektur excellent (DESIGN.md 1975 lines)
+ Arena allocator suite (bump, typed, slab, pool)
+ Cache Manager dengan content hashing
+ Multiple frontend (CLI + Tauri GUI)
+ Open source MIT license

RECOMMENDATION:

Maria harus di-reposisi sebagai:
1. Fast prototyping/syntax-checking tool untuk SV engineers
2. Educational platform untuk memahami simulator architecture
3. Research platform untuk compiler optimization pada HDL
4. Reference implementation untuk IEEE 1800 scheduling

TIDAK UNTUK:
1. Tape-out verification
2. Gate-level simulation
3. Formal sign-off
4. Industrial regression
5. Commercial deployment tanpa 5+ tahun development tambahan

Untuk mencapai enterprise readiness, rekomendasi fokus immediate:
- ✅ CI/CD + all tests fixed — SELESAI
- Code coverage instrumentation (6 months)
- Compiled code simulation via Cranelift (12 months)
- LSP server (6 months)
- Full UVM 1.2 (12 months)
- VPI standard (12 months)
- ✅ SAT-based constraint solver starter: domain analysis solver (done)
- Full SAT solver integration (minisat/kissat) — if needed for complex arithmetic
- Race condition + X-propagation detection (3 months)

   - ENHANCED: konstanta negatif two's complement yang muat di LHS signed juga
     di-skip (reg signed [7:0] x = -1 → 0xFF, x = -128 → 0x80, TANPA warning);
     konstanta yang benar-benar tidak muat (mis. -200 dalam signed [7:0]) tetap
     warning — diverifikasi via flush_diagnostics (test_negative_const_width_warning_suppression)
   - Files: src/elaboration/elaborator/stmt.rs
   - DOCUMENTED LIMITATION: konstanta unsigned besar ber-MSB-set (mis.
     32'hFFFFFF80) akan terdeteksi sebagai negatif karena fold tidak menyimpan
     info tanda (inheren two's complement)
   - DOCUMENTED LIMITATION: `$bits` pada signal array unpacked lokal (mis.
     `reg [7:0] arr[0:3]`) over-count (128 bukan 32) — info.width sudah
     termasuk dimensi array, lalu dikali array_depth lagi di jalur
     resolve_expr_signal (bug PRE-EXISTING, di luar scope fix param package;
     diverifikasi via test: $bits(arr) = 128 vs 32 yang benar)

✅ ELAB: `$bits` referensi array utuh (param package)
   - try_array_param_bits() di expr.rs: resolve `Expr::ScopedIdent`
     (`$bits(pkg::ARR)`) atau plain-name via import (`$bits(ARR)` dengan
     `import pkg::*`) ke param array package, lalu hitung
     elem_width × jumlah elemen (dari pkg_const_arrays + ParamDecl dtype/range)
   - `$bits(COEFFS)` = `$bits(cfg_pkg::COEFFS)` = 128 (4 int × 32 bit) —
     menghapus documented limitation lama "nama array utuh tidak ter-resolve"
   - DRY: collect_import_sets() diekstrak, dipakai try_array_param_bits dan
     elaborate_imported_package_func_call (hilangkan duplikasi blok identik)
   - Files: src/elaboration/elaborator/expr.rs

✅ ELAB: $bits/$size signal array — hapus double-count + $size dimensi pertama
   - $bits pada signal: SignalInfo.width sudah lebar total (elem_width × depth
     di-set saat elaborasi), jadi `info.width * array_depth` double-count.
     Kini cukup `info.width` — `$bits(reg [7:0] arr[0:3])` = 32 (bukan 128)
   - $size pada unpacked array: kini mengembalikan jumlah elemen dimensi
     pertama (array_depth, = 4 untuk [0:3]), bukan lebar total (32)
   - compute_expr_width (width.rs) disamakan: Expr::Ident → info.width (konsisten
     dengan handler $bits, perbaiki koneksi port array yang over-size)
   - Files: src/elaboration/elaborator/expr.rs, src/elaboration/util/width.rs
   - ENHANCED: $size packed multi-dimensi kini packed_dims[0] —
     `$size(reg [3:0][7:0] p)` = 4 (jumlah elemen dimensi pertama), bukan 32
     (lebar total). Prioritas: array_depth (unpacked) > packed_dims[0] > width
   - DOCUMENTED LIMITATION: $size pada array 1-elemen [0:0] (array_depth=1)
     kembali ke lebar total (8) karena tak bisa dibedakan dari non-array
     (array_dims selalu kosong)
   - DOCUMENTED LIMITATION: $size pada kombinasi packed multi-dimensi + unpacked
     array (mis. `reg [7:0][3:0] arr[0:3]`) memprioritaskan array_depth (4),
     padahal IEEE 1800 mengharuskan dimensi paling kiri ([7:0] → 8) — edge jarang
     (catatan: limitation $bits PORT unpacked-array telah DIHAPUS — fixed di
      ROUND 12 via fold array depth di jalur port)

═══ ROUND 84 — WAV-16 mwave decode + FIX BUG ENGINE & EMU RTL CPU (2026-08-24) ═══

✅ WAV-16 PROTOCOL-AWARE WAVEFORM DECODE — DIIMPLEMENTASIKAN (baru):
   - crates/maria-tools/src/wave.rs: subcommand `decode` — dekode transaksi
     bus dari timeline VCD. Tiga protokol:
     • apb      — SETUP (psel&!penable → latch addr/wdata/dir) → ACCESS
                  selesai saat pready=1; pready absen = selalu ready;
                  wait-state (pready=0) ditangani (transaksi selesai saat
                  pready naik, rdata di-sample di cycle itu).
     • axi4lite — handshake valid/ready per channel: write aw+w→b, read
                  ar→r; resp bresp/rresp tercatat di DecodedTx.resp;
                  transaksi merge-sort by end-time lalu re-index.
     • ahb      — AHB-Lite single transfer: address phase NONSEQ("10")/
                  SEQ("11") dgn hready=1, data phase selesai di hready=1
                  berikutnya; pipelining back-to-back (addr phase baru pada
                  cycle yang sama dgn data phase sebelumnya) ditangani.
   - Helper: find_signal (nama komponen terakhir, fallback case-insensitive
     → sinyal di scope nested tetap ketemu), timeline_of, sample_at (nilai
     @t = perubahan terakhir ≤ t), bits_val, union_times.
   - CLI: MwaveCmd::Decode { input, --proto apb|axi4lite|ahb } (cli.rs +
     dispatch main.rs). AGENTS.md mwave row di-update.
   - Demo e2e .sv dulu (sesuai aturan): apb_demo.sv (task apb_write/apb_read,
     VCD via maria run) → decode 4 transaksi benar (WRITE 0x10=0xAB,
     WRITE 0x24=0x3C, READ 0x40=0x51, READ 0x44=0x55); axi_demo.sv → 3 tx
     (2 WRITE + 1 READ dgn resp OKAY); ahb_demo.sv → 2 tx benar. Jalur error
     proto tak dikenal + sinyal hilang terverifikasi pesan jelas.
   - Test resmi (5 unit inline wave.rs): test_apb_decode_write_read_and_wait_
     state (termasuk wait-state pready), test_axi4lite_decode_write_and_read_
     channels (resp OKAY/SLVERR), test_ahb_decode_single_transfers,
     test_decode_missing_signal_errors, test_sample_at_and_bits_val.

✅ FIX BUG — RtlLinkedCpu bus protocol (8 test cpu::rtl FAIL/hang → PASS):
   Gejala: test_rtl_cpu_runs_elf_program dkk gagal (nilai salah) +
   test_rtl_cpu_irq_timer/irq_uart_tx hang >60s.
   Akar masalah 3 lapis:
   1. PROTOCOL VIOLATION (rtl.rs): picorv32 memakai stream valid/ready —
      `mem_valid` TETAP tinggi utk transaksi back-to-back. Host menunggu
      `!mem_valid` sbg penyelesaian → tidak pernah terjadi setelah txn
      pertama → mem_ready mentok 1 → semua fetch berikutnya selesai instan
      dgn mem_rdata BASI (mem_rdata_q stuck di instruksi pertama; CPU
      eksekusi instruksi sama berulang sambil prefetch jalan). Fix:
      penyelesaian non-MMIO deterministik — serve (poke ready+rdata) → posedge
      berikutnya = selesai (close ready, catat fetch) → transaksi baru bisa
      dilayani di tick yang sama; MMIO (decoder RTL yang ack) tetap deteksi
      valid turun. Field served_mmio baru.
   2. SHIFT CONTEXT WIDTH (engine, global): `decoded_imm <= mem_rdata_q[31:12]
      << 12` dievaluasi dalam lebar operand 20-bit → semua bit high terbuang
      (LUI menghasilkan 0 bukan 0x80000000). IEEE 1800-2017 §11.6: width
      ekspresi RHS assignment = max(width LHS, self-determined). Fix:
      eval_assign_rhs kini evaluasi via evaluate_expr_ctx(expr, width_lhs) —
      operand shift di-extend ke width konteks SEBELUM digeser (Sshr signed =
      sign-extend via is_signed_expr, sisanya zero; dispatch >>> signed/
      unsigned identik dgn jalur evaluate_expr_impl). Berlaku global untuk
      SEMUA assignment (blocking/NBA/continuous).
   3. pc() SEMANTIK: picorv32 mem-prefetch 1 instruksi MELEWATI ebreak →
      alamat fetch terakhir host = ebreak+4. Fix: CpuPorts.reg_pc (opsional,
      probe `u_cpu.reg_pc`) — pc() membaca reg_pc internal RTL bila tersedia,
      fallback last_pc.
   Hasil: 8/8 test cpu::rtl pass (compile/ports, runs_elf, mmio_uart_console,
   mmio_read_status, irq_timer, irq_uart_tx, uart_rx_bidirectional,
   reset_pc_starts_at_reset_vector) — maria-emu 70/70 pass.

✅ FIX BUG — test_resize_extend (maria-simulator packed_eval): test menuntut
   extend X padahal PackedLogicVec::resize zero-extend by design (dokumentasi
   + konsisten dgn LogicVec::resize + semantik assignment Verilog). Test
   diperbaiki (bits[4]/bits[7] = Zero), bukan implementasinya.

✅ CLEANUP — hapus crates/maria-tests/examples/mdme_run.rs: import
   `maria_tests::mdme` yang TIDAK PERNAH ada di tree (modul mdme tidak
   pernah di-commit) → example mati permanen dan memblokir
   `cargo test --workspace` (E0432).

═══ ROUND 86 — WAV-19: VCD background writer thread ═══

WAV-19 (VCD writer single-threaded, blocking sim during dump, High) ditutup:
   - crates/maria-simulator/src/waveform/vcd.rs (refactor jalur tulis):
     • enum Out { File(fs::File) | Bg(BgWriter) | Detached } menggantikan
       field `file` langsung. Detached = placeholder transien saat
       mengambil File dari self.out di enable_background().
     • BgWriter { tx: Option<Sender<BgMsg>>, handle: JoinHandle } +
       enum BgMsg { Bytes(Vec<u8>) | Flush(Sender<()>) }. Writer thread
       ("maria-vcd-writer") pemilik eksklusif file via BufWriter: loop
       recv → write_all; Flush → flush + kirim ack; channel tertutup →
       flush final + exit.
     • enable_background() (opt-in, pub): pindahkan file ke thread,
       semua dump berikutnya non-blocking bagi sim (backpressure alami
       saat buffer channel penuh).
     • flush(): File → f.flush(); Bg → Msg::Flush + tunggu ack (setelah
       flush() kembali semua antrean sudah di-disk). Thread mati →
       enabled = false (anti silent-loss data).
     • close_inner(): drop tx → join handle → file final. reopen()
       memakai jalur ini sebelum buat file baru.
     • Drop impl: flush() + close_inner() — drop tanpa close tetap
       mem-finalisasi file (DEBT-19 diperluas ke mode background).
     • Semua writeln!(self.file, ...) di header ($scope/$var/$upscope)
       diroute lewat write_raw agar konsisten lewat sink aktif.
   - src/cli.rs: flag --waveform-bg.
   - src/main.rs: enable_background() wired di KEDUA jalur (run legacy
     + run_fast) setelah VcdWriter::new, dengan report status.

Test (+3 unit inline vcd.rs):
   - test_background_mode_writes_identical_output — dump sinkron vs
     background (dump_all + time header + dump_state) → isi file
     IDENTIK byte-per-byte.
   - test_background_flush_synchronizes — flush() sinkron; setelah
     kembali header + nilai data=7 sudah terbaca dari disk.
   - test_background_drop_finalizes_file — drop TANPA close() → Drop
     impl join thread; b00000011 terbaca di file.

Demo e2e CLI: demo.sv --waveform-bg → "Waveform background writer
enabled"; cmp bg.vcd sync.vcd → IDENTIK.

   - cargo build --workspace: 0 error
   - cargo test --workspace: 2129 passed, 0 failed (+3)

✅ VERIF-32 SEQUENCE COVERAGE — DIIMPLEMENTASIKAN:

     failed} + field engine.sequence_coverage HashMap<(line,col), SeqCovStats>.
   - coverage.rs: record_sequence_attempt() (dipanggil di KEDUA situs push
     SequenceAttempt di block.rs) + record_sequence_result() (dipanggil saat
     attempt selesai di sequence.rs, berdampingan record_assertion VERIF-27)
     + report_sequence_coverage() (per line:col attempts/matched/failed +
     penanda [HOLE] + match rate; dipanggil report_full_coverage) +
     integration coverage_gaps(): sequence matched==0 → gap "tidak pernah
     match" + coverage_stats(): sequence_tracked/attempts/matched/failed/
     match_percent (mengalir ke Coverage Summary msim + cache pipeline).
   - Test e2e maria-tests test_sequence_coverage_matched_and_hole: `a ##1 b`
     match sekali + fail sekali → stats tercatat; design kedua yang tidak
     pernah match → hole terdeteksi + muncul di coverage_gaps(). Mutation-
     verified: hook attempt dimatikan → test FAIL.

⚠️ BUG GLOBAL DITEMUKAN & DIPERBAIKI — FastLexer TIDAK menghasilkan
   Token::HashHash untuk `##`:
   Gejala: module-level `assert property (@(posedge clk) a ##1 b)` bekerja
   di jalur CLI legacy (concatenate + Lexer legacy) tapi HILANG diam-diam
   di seluruh jalur CompileSession: `maria sim` (msim), melab/mlint/
   filelist run_fast/LSP. Assertion Summary failed=0 padahal property
   harusnya fail; sequence/assertion coverage kosong.
   Akar masalah: frontend/lexer.rs FastLexer arm b'#' SELALU → Token::Hash
   (dua kali untuk `##`). Parser menerima `a # # 1 b` → parse_immediate_
   assertion gagal → jalur rollback ROUND 71 me-skip assertion SENGAJA
   (desain anti-crash) → PropertyAssert hilang dari AST/IR.
   Fix: FastLexer b'#' peek '#' → konsumsi dua byte → Token::HashHash
   (paritas lexer legacy lexer.rs:1283). `#` tunggal (delay) tetap Hash.
   Dampak fix: SEMUA SVA temporal (`##N`, `##[min:max]`, concat/or/and/
   repeat/implication) kini hidup kembali di jalur CompileSession —
   terverifikasi e2e: msim sequence_tracked=1 (8 attempts / 0 matched /
   5 failed), filelist run_fast menampilkan Sequence Coverage + Coverage
   Gaps [HOLE], identik dengan jalur legacy.
   Test regresi: test_fast_lexer_hashhash_temporal_delay (maria-compiler
   lexer.rs) — `a ##1 b` → HashHash + Number; `#5` tetap Hash.

═══ ROUND 88 — Mem-03/PERF-17 mmap retention + PERF-02 stale ═══

✅ Mem-03 + PERF-17 — MMAP RETENTION DIHAPUS (frontend/io.rs):
   Temuan: MmapFile MENAHAN region `Mmap` di struct (field dead_code)
   PADAHAL isinya sudah di-copy ke heap `bytes` sejak awal → (1) memori
   dobel per file besar (mapped pages + heap copy), (2) address space
   tertahan selama MmapFile hidup tanpa batas jumlah file concurrent
   (akar klaim Mem-03 "100K files = 1GB virtual" dan PERF-17 "mmaps
   held open during compile").
   Fix: mapping dilepas SEGERA setelah salinan dibuat:
     - File >=4KB (MMAP_THRESHOLD): mmap → advise Sequential → copy →
       drop map (unmap otomatis). I/O tetap secepat mmap utk file besar.
     - File kecil: read biasa (tidak berubah).
   - track_mmap_bytes() kini benar-benar terpanggil di jalur mmap-copy.
   - Zero-copy penuh tetap ada di jalur MICD (micd/format.rs) yang
     membaca database ter-map dengan scope terbatas & terkelola.
   - Test: test_large_file_no_map_retained (file >=4KB konten utuh +
     checksum xxh3 atas salinan heap) + test_small_file_read_directly
     (pengganti test_small_file_no_mmap yang mengakses field mmap).

✅ PERF-02 — RE-VERIFIKASI STALE → [x]:
   Signal history sudah VecDeque O(1) pop_front sejak ROUND 9, kini
   SignalHistoryStore ring buffer per-signal (default 10K) + disk spill
   opsional (CRIT-019/Mem-01). Marker audit tidak pernah diupdate.

═══ ROUND 91 — FORMAL-03/04 k-INDUCTION (TAHAP 1) + 3 FIX BUG FORMAL ═══

Kerja bertahap pada FORMAL-03 (induction proof) & FORMAL-04 (k-induction)
— inti algoritma SELESAI, item tetap ⚠️ IN PROGRESS (scope penuh: SVA
concurrent property, liveness, IC3/PDR masih open).

✅ k-INDUCTION UMUM (crates/maria-formal/bmc.rs):
   - check_inductive digeneralisasi: iterasi k = 1..config.max_k (default
     8). Per iterasi: bangun k+1 frame F0..Fk TANPA initial constraint,
     hubungkan via relasi transisi, ASSUME P(F0)..P(Fk-1), CHECK ¬P(Fk).
     UNSAT → InductiveProof(k); SAT → spurious, coba k lebih dalam.
   - enum InductionOutcome { Proved | Spurious | Inconclusive | Error }.
   - FormalResult::InductiveProof → InductiveProof(k) (bawa kedalaman
     bukti). FormalConfig + field max_k.
   - CLI: --formal-induction (cli.rs) wired ke run_formal (main.rs);
     report mencetak "✓ INDUCTIVE PROOF (k=N)".

✅ 3 BUG BLOCKING DITEMUKAN & DIPERBAIKI saat verifikasi CLI:
   1. init X/Z dipaksa 0: sinyal tanpa initializer punya init_val X-fill;
      to_u64() membaca X=0 → constraint state awal SALAH → false FAIL di
      depth 0. Fix: init yang mengandung X/Z dibiarkan unconstrained
      (abstraksi 2-state sound), hanya init terdefinisi penuh di-constrain.
   2. Initializer deklarasi hilang dari relasi transisi: `reg x = 1;`
      di-elaborasi menjadi proses Initial `decl_init_*`, tidak pernah
      masuk BMC. Fix: collect_initial_state_assignments() — BlockingAssign
      leading proses Initial jadi constraint sig_0 == rhs (walk berhenti
      di statement non-assign).
   3. IrExpr::Signed tidak diterjemahkan ke Z3: sejak fix signedness
      ROUND 36, literal desimal unsized dibungkus Signed → expr_to_z3_
      int_at mengembalikan None → constraint/assertion DI-SKIP diam-diam.
      Fix: arm IrExpr::Signed delegasi ke inner (semantik BV sama).

✅ Test baru (+1): test_k_induction_needs_depth_2 — invariant a|b dengan
   transisi a'=b, b'=b, init (0,1): TIDAK terbukti di k=1 (spurious),
   TERBUKTI di k=2 → membuktikan iterative deepening bekerja.

E2e CLI (--formal --formal-induction):
   - f_simple.sv (assert x==1): ✓ INDUCTIVE PROOF (k=1)
   - formal_k2.sv (a||b, butuh k>=2): ✓ INDUCTIVE PROOF (k=2)

═══ ROUND 26 — DROP IMPL + DEBUG KONSISTEN (DEBT-19 + DEBT-20) ═══

✅ DEBT-19: Drop impl untuk shared resources (waveform writers)
   - Sebelumnya hanya CsvWaveWriter yang punya Drop (flush). VcdWriter dan
     FstWaveWriter tidak punya Drop → file bisa bocor tak ter-flush jika
     engine return early (mis. error sim) tanpa close() eksplisit.
   - src/waveform/vcd.rs: impl Drop for VcdWriter → self.flush(). flush()
     ekuivalen dengan close() (close_inner hanya flush), menghindari
     double-flush — pola sama dengan CsvWaveWriter.
   - src/waveform/fst.rs: impl Drop for FstWaveWriter → self.close(). close()
     memakai writer.take() sebelum finish() → idempoten, finish() hanya
     sekali. FST baru benar-benar ter-flush saat finish(), jadi Drop ini
     kritis untuk mencegah file FST terpotong.
   - Catatan: close_csv() sudah ada di engine; jalur normal main.rs tidak
     memanggil close_vcd/close_fst eksplisit — Drop adalah safety net.

✅ DEBT-20: Debug/Deref konsisten di tipe waveform
   - VcdWriter: awalnya #[derive(Debug)] (membuang isi penuh map
     last_values/code_by_key — bisa raksasa untuk desain besar), atas saran
     reviewer diganti impl std::fmt::Debug manual ringkas: enabled,
     max_dump_size, total_written, stream_flush_interval, signal_count,
     code_count.
   - FstWaveWriter: impl std::fmt::Debug manual — writer_active, var_count,
     current_time, enabled, stream_flush_interval (FstWriter wavefst tidak
     implement Debug, jadi tidak bisa derive; tidak menyentuh field raw

═══ ROUND 29 — WAIT FORK (LANG-29) + RESTORE PROSES PADA RESUME ═══

✅ LANG-29 WAIT FORK — DIIMPLEMENTASIKAN (baru):
   - AST: Stmt::WaitFork (fieldless, maria-ast/src/stmt.rs) — diproduksi
     parser untuk `wait fork` (Token::Fork, BUKAN Ident — fork keyword),
     berbeda dari `wait (0)` yang tetap Stmt::Wait{cond,stmt}.
   - IR: IrStmt::WaitFork (maria-ir/src/ir.rs); elaborator stmt.rs &
     flatten.rs pass-through; inline.rs/inline_util.rs arm no-op;
     synth subset.rs SYN-2 (non-synthesizable) + walker; sim_dag.rs
     (tanpa akses signal); lint.rs scan_stmt_reads arm.
   - Engine: ForkGroup.spawner = current_process_name saat fork dibuat
     (types.rs); active_fork_groups_for_current_process() memilih group
     active & remaining>0 milik proses berjalan (JoinAny selesai
     remaining=0 tidak ikut); pending_wait_forks (WaitForkState) berisi
     fids + kontinuasi IR/AST + konteks method + process_name;
     check_wait_forks(fid) dipanggil di fork_finish → resume kontinuasi
     saat SEMUA fid yang ditunggu selesai. Tanpa group aktif → lanjut
     segera (semantik LRM: tidak ada child = tidak menunggu).
   - Fix arsitektural terkait: current_process_name TIDAK di-restore saat
     resume ContinueBlock (hanya di-set di EvalProcess t=0) → wait fork
     setelah `#2` melihat nama proses lain (spawner mismatch). Fix:
     Continuation.process_name disimpan saat suspend (2 situs delay di
     block.rs) dan di-restore di ContinueBlock (scheduler/event.rs).
     Berlaku global — fitur lain berbasis nama proses (mis. coverage
     record_line_hit per proses) ikut benar setelah resume delay.
   - Test BARU (3): test_wait_fork_waits_for_join_none (snapshot = done1*10
     + done2 = 11 → membuktikan kontinuasi jalan SETELAH kedua branch
     selesai, bukan segera), test_wait_fork_no_active_fork_continues,
     test_wait_fork_nested_in_fork_branch (wait fork di dalam branch fork
     menunggu child-nya; join luar tetap menunggu semua branch).
   - Verifikasi manual: scratch tb `fork #5/#10 join_none; #2; wait fork`
     → t=2 sebelum wait fork done=0, t=10 setelah wait fork done=1,1 ✓

✅ LANG-30 DISABLE FORK — DIIMPLEMENTASIKAN (baru):

     dengan `wait fork` yang sudah diperbaiki di ROUND 29).

     (block.rs: fork path, stmt_block path, AST path) — name=="fork" →
     tandai group aktif milik proses (reuse active_fork_groups_for_current_process)
     lalu LANJUT; name!=fork → perilaku lama (disable_pending, kill blok
     bernama); ContinueBlock/ContinueAstBlock (scheduler/event.rs) me-skip
     eksekusi branch disabled + fork_decrement → branch mati tanpa body,
     group mencapai remaining 0 → join/join_none finalisasi normal.

     (perlu named fork di parser/IR).

═══ ROUND 36 lanjutan — VHPI/PLI ADAPTER (arsitektur masukan user) ═══

VHPI (IEEE 1076-2008) + PLI (IEEE 1364) diimplementasikan sebagai
ABI-compatible adapter di atas simulation kernel Maria, mengikuti urutan
yang disarankan user: foreign ABI infra → loader → handle registry → value
→ callback/event → VHPI → PLI.

1. foreign/ (Maria FFI unified — crates/maria-simulator/src/foreign/):
   - handle.rs: ForeignHandle (u64 id + HandleKind) + HandleRegistry
     (Box<dyn Any>) — library eksternal TIDAK pernah menerima pointer
     internal Maria (poin 6 user); clear di end-of-sim.
   - loader.rs: candidate_paths (libfoo.so/foo.so/libfoo.dylib/foo.dll),
     find_library (cari di cwd/LD_LIBRARY_PATH//usr/lib...), resolve_symbol
     (T: Copy), AbiInfo (arch/os/pointer_width) — poin 2, 9, 10.
   - mod.rs: ForeignEvent (ValueChange/ReadWriteSync/ReadOnlySync/
     NextTimeStep/Callback/EndOfSimulation) — semua callback foreign masuk
     antrian scheduler utama (poin 5), bukan jalan sendiri.
2. vhpi/ (IEEE 1076-2008, konstanta dari vhpi_user.h — ABI asli, bukan
   versi Maria sendiri): object.rs (vhpi_handle_by_name/vhpi_get/
   vhpi_get_str/vhpi_is_defined + engine hook set_vhpi_engine),
   handle.rs (VhpiHandle u64 + registry + vhpi_release_handle),
   value.rs (t_vhpi_value union + vhpiIntVal/LogicVal/BinStrVal ↔
   LogicVec), iterator.rs (vhpi_iterate/vhpi_scan), callback.rs
   (register/remove unique id + dispatch start/end/time-step/synch),
   api.rs (#[no_mangle] extern "C": vhpi_handle_by_name/vhpi_get_value/
   vhpi_put_value/vhpi_control/vhpi_register_cb...), loader.rs
   (load_vhpi_library + call_vhpi_startup + vhpi_cleanup).
3. pli/ (IEEE 1364 PLI 1.0): tf.rs (tf_getinstance/tf_getp/tf_putp/
   tf_getlongp/tf_putlongp/tf_strgetp/tf_strputp/tf_sizep/tf_gettime +
   plio_warning/plio_error — registry argumen per-instance u32),
   acc.rs (acc_initialize/acc_handle_signal/acc_handle_tfarg/
   acc_fetch_value/acc_fetch_name/acc_fetch_fullname/acc_fetch_type/
   acc_next/acc_close), loader.rs (load_pli_library + veriusertfs/
   vpi_startup + has_pli_entry_points + pli_cleanup).
4. Engine hooks (core.rs run()): set_vhpi_engine + dispatch start-of-sim
   di awal; dispatch end-of-sim + vhpi_cleanup + pli_cleanup di akhir.
5. Test: 33 unit murni Rust (foreign 7: handle roundtrip/null/clear +
   loader paths/find/abi; vhpi 18: object constants/is_defined/handle_by_
   name-null, handle roundtrip/null/clear, value int/logic/binstr/
   ptr/null, iterator scan, callback register/reason-filter/remove-
   middle/remove-null, loader abi/missing; pli 8: tf getput/bitvec/
   instance-time/bits_u64 + acc format/fetch-null + loader abi/missing)
   + 2 e2e maria-tests (test_vhpi_engine_hook_e2e: vhpi_handle_by_name
   menemukan signal "count" saat start-of-sim callback; test_pli_tf_e2e_
   via_sim: sim tanpa crash PLI cleanup + tf_getinstance/tf_gettime).
   Total +35 test.

═══ ROUND 36 — DUA BUG GLOBAL: LITERAL NEGATIF SIGNED + MICD IR CACHE STALE ═══

✅ BUG 1 — Literal negatif berperilaku unsigned di SEMUA jalur (temuan
   ROUND 34, `compute(-5)` → 4294967291; `a=-5; a<0` → false):
   Akar masalah 3 lapis, semuanya diperbaiki global (IR + AST):
   1. is_signed_type (elaboration/util/type_util.rs) hanya true utk
      DataType::Signed(_) — padahal IEEE 1800 §6.11: int/integer/byte/
      shortint/longint intrinsik SIGNED. Signal `int a` jadi is_signed=
      false → perbandingan memakai eval_binary unsigned → `a < 0` = false
      (salah). Fix: matches! tambah Int/Integer/Byte/Shortint/Longint.
   2. try_fold_const (elaboration/util/const_fold.rs) mem-fold `-5` jadi
      IrExpr::Const(0xFFFFFFFB) TANPA Signed — signedness hilang saat
      konstanta, sehingga `-5 < 0` di-fill unsigned. Fix: val<0 → bungkus
      IrExpr::Signed(Const(...)) (konsisten dgn `32'sd-5`).
   3. format_display %d (simulator/util.rs) selalu to_u64() — display tak
      pernah negatif. Fix: signedness per-arg dibawa (is_signed_expr utk
      jalur IR; ast_expr_is_signed utk jalur AST `-<literal>`) → %d signed
      mencetak -5, bukan 4294967291.
   Fix 1 memicu 1 regresi test (test_negative_const_width_warning_suppression:
   RHS `-1` kini Signed(Const) bukan Const → suppression width warning tidak
   match). Fix: check_width_mismatch (elaborator/stmt.rs) unwrap Signed(Const).
   Test BARU: test_negative_literal_signed_semantics (maria-tests) — a<0
   true, -5<0 true, compute(-5) bits benar, r1==-5 true.

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — Div/Mod SIGNED + parallel eval konsisten:
   4. eval_binary_signed (simulator/value.rs) hanya menangani Lt/Le/Gt/Ge
      — Div/Mod selalu eval_binary UNSIGNED: `int a=-7; a/2` = 2147483644
      (harusnya -3), `-7%-2` = 0 (harusnya 3? -7%-2=-1). Fix: arm Div/Mod
      memakai i64 wrapping_div/rem (truncate toward zero = semantik SV
      §11.4.3) + evaluate_expr (eval/expr.rs) memasukkan Div/Mod ke kondisi
      is_signed_expr. Verifikasi: -7/2=-3, -7%2=-1, -7/-2=3, 7%-2=-3/1.
   5. Jalur parallel eval (parallel.rs, default ON utk Process::Combinational
      ≥4 proses) memakai eval_binary langsung → signed div/mod TIDAK
      konsisten dgn serial. Fix: evaluate_expr_simple / evaluate_stmt_block_
      parallel / eval_assign_rhs_simple / get_lvalue_width_simple /
      write_lvalue_simple kini menerima &[SignalInfo] dan memakai is_signed_
      expr + eval_binary_signed (sama dgn jalur serial). Caller: event.rs
      (parallel trigger) pass self.design.top.signals; sim_dag.rs (dead
      code — exported tanpa caller) pass &[].
   Test BARU: test_signed_division_modulo (maria-tests) — q1=-3, m1=-1,
   q2=3, m2=-1, q3=-3, m3=1.

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — `>>>` (Sshr) semantik signed/unsigned:
   6. eval_binary Sshr selalu arithmetic + extend_to ZERO-extend ke max_width
      operand → sign bit lebar ASLI hilang: `logic signed [7:0] s = -128;
      s >>> 2` = 0x20 (harus 0xE0). IEEE 1800 §11.4.10: >>> arithmetic bila
      lhs SIGNED, logical bila unsigned. Fix: eval_sshr_signed (value.rs)
      memakai lebar asli lhs + pemilihan di evaluate_expr (eval/expr.rs) &
      parallel path (parallel.rs): signed → eval_sshr_signed, unsigned →
      eval_binary(Shr). Verifikasi: signed -128>>>2=0xE0, unsigned 0x80>>>2=
      0x20, >> selalu logical, shift≥width → sign fill 0xFF, int -8>>>2=-2
      (parallel juga).
   Test BARU: test_arithmetic_shift_right_signedness (maria-tests).

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — signedness PROPAGASI ke ekspresi majemuk:
   7. is_signed_expr (simulator/util.rs) hanya true utk Signal/Signed wrapper
      — `int a; (a+1) < 0` memakai unsigned compare (a=-5 → a+1=-4 < 0 =
      false, salah). Fix: rekursi BinaryOp/UnaryOp/Cond/ExprRangeSelect/
      ExprBitSelect — hasil operasi signed bila salah satu operand signed
      (aturan `||` eksisting; catatan LRM §11.8.2 'ada operand unsigned →
      hasil unsigned' TIDAK diterapkan karena IR Const tidak menyimpan info
      literal signed/unsized — keterbatasan dicatat). Berlaku juga utk
      div/mod, `>>>`, dan %d display pd ekspresi majemuk.
   Test BARU: test_compound_expr_signedness_propagation (maria-tests) —
   (a+1)<0=true, (a*2)<0=true, (-a)<0=false, (a+1)/2=-3, (a*2)>>>2=-4.

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — aturan LRM §11.8.2 PENUH (any-unsigned → unsigned):
   8. Keputusan operasi di evaluator diganti `||` → `&&` (signed hanya bila
      KEDUA operand signed). Dua prasyarat baru:
      a. Literal desimal UNSIZED (`0`, `127`, `2`) di-emit elaborator sebagai
         IrExpr::Signed (LRM §6.8.1: unsized constant numbers signed) —
         sebelumnya selalu Const unsigned.
      b. eval_binary_signed kini SIGN-extend operan dari lebar ASLINYA
         (helper sign_extend_to; extend_to lama zero-extend) —
         `logic signed [7:0] s = -1; s < 0` = -1 < 0 = true, bukan 255 < 0
         (bug pra-ada yang tersembunyi karena int/int sama lebar).
      Efek: `s < 8'h7F` → UNSIGNED (hex sized tanpa suffix s) = 0xFF < 0x7F
      = false (LRM benar; sebelumnya signed = -1 < 127 = true).
      `a / 8'h02` → unsigned division. `a < 0` dan `a / 2` tetap signed
      (0 dan 2 desimal → Signed). Parallel path konsisten (&& + sign-extend
      via eval_binary_signed). Full suite 1809 → tanpa regresi.
   Test BARU: test_lrm_signedness_any_unsigned_rule (maria-tests) — 7 kasus
   (s<0, s<8'h7F, s<127, u>8'hFE, a<0, a<8'h02, s/8'h02).

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — signedness hasil CONST-FOLD (operand asli):
   9. try_fold_const (const_fold.rs) membungkus fold NEGATIF saja (`val < 0`)
      — hasil fold POSITIF selalu Const unsigned → `int a; a < (2+3)` salah
      unsigned (2+3 fold → Const(5) unsigned → 0xFFFFFFFB < 5 = false).
      Fix: helper const_expr_is_signed(expr) — signed bila SEMUA literal
      operand signed (desimal unsized §6.8.1 / suffix s), unsigned bila ada
      satu Binary/Hex/Octal tanpa s (any-unsigned §11.8.2); Ident/param tidak
      dilacak → konservatif unsigned (keterbatasan dicatat).
      `a < (2+3)` → signed ✓; `a < (8'h01+8'h05)` → unsigned ✓; sekaligus
      memperbaiki kasus `8'h01 - 8'h05` (sebelumnya `val<0` salah bungkus
      Signed padahal operand unsigned).
   Test BARU: test_const_fold_signedness_propagation (maria-tests).
   KETERBATASAN dicatat: ekspresi konstanta yang SELURUHNYA ter-fold
   dievaluasi const_eval_with_params di domain i64 tanpa sadar-lebar/sign
   (mis. `(8'h01-8'h05) < 0` = -4 < 0 = 1, padahal LRM: 8'hFC=252 < 0 = 0;
   `8'h06 / 8'h02` hasil -3 utk negatif). Butuh evaluator konstanta
   sadar-lebar — di luar scope round ini.

✅ BUG 1-lanjutan — SOLVER CONSTRAINT signed (UVM randomize):
   10. Solver (uvm/constraint_solver.rs) memakai u64 & masking tanpa
       signedness — `rand int x; constraint { x < 0; }` domain narrowing
       dan validasi salah utk nilai negatif:
       a. `a as i64` pd bit MASKED (0xFFFFFFFB utk -5) memberi 4294967291
          BUKAN -5 → inside RangeSelect `[-5:5]` diurutkan lo=5 hi=
          0xFFFFFFFB (interval kontigu 4-miliar, praktis mustahil dipenuhi
          guided). Fix: helper sign_extend(bits, width) + urutan min/max
          via sign_extend di arm RangeSelect/BinaryOp/Dist-Range; hasil
          InsideRange WRAP (lo > hi) = [lo, max] ∪ [0, hi].
       b. merge_min/merge_max membandingkan `c as i64` pd masked bits →
          salah utk signed. Fix: terima width, bandingkan via sign_extend.
       c. signed_range full_max = (1<<W)-1 = 0xFF..F yang di-domain signed
          = -1, BUKAN +2^(W-1)-1 → `x > -10` menge-cap max ke -1 → nilai
          positif tak pernah bisa dipilih utk x. Fix: full_max =
          (1<<(W-1))-1 = 0x7F..F (BUG PRA-ADA tersembunyi).
       d. generate() tidak menangani interval WRAP (lo > hi) utk inside &
          bounds → total/offset salah (underflow u64). Fix: interval_count/
          interval_value baru (wrap = [lo, max] ∪ [0, hi]).
       e. Validasi `x dist {[-5:5] := 1, ...}` memakai to_u64 range →
          z=-3 (0xFFFFFFFD) > 4294967291 = false → dist negatif tak pernah
          valid di combined constraints. Fix: signed → to_i64 range check.
       f. try_extract_u64 TIDAK bisa parse hex `8'hF0` (bits.parse::<u64>()
          desimal) → domain `u > 8'hF0` KOSONG → rejection 32-bit praktis
          mustahil di combined. Fix: u64::from_str_radix per radix literal.
       g. Single value NEGATIF `w inside {7, -3, 42}` — `-3` (UnaryOp
          Minus) jatuh ke `_ => return false` → analisis di-putus di tengah
          (domain parsial). Fix: arm UnaryOp via extract_bits.
   Verifikasi e2e: 6 constraint digabung 1 class — x∈(-10,100) signed
   (mixed bound), y∈[-200,-100], z∈dist{-5..5,100}, w∈{7,-3,42}, u>8'hF0,
   v∈[0x20,0x30] → randomize()=1 semua nilai valid (serial + guided).
   Test BARU: test_constraint_signed_domains (maria-tests) — 6 kasus,
   nilai diverifikasi via to_i64/to_u64 di sisi test.

BUG 1 — Nested struct member access (rvalue + width typedef struct):

   menjadi MemberAccess{obj:RangeSelect} yang di-interpretasi engine sbg

      dari `compute_struct_fields` (lebar per member via range→typedef→
      nested struct), bukan `dtype.width()`. → `hw2reg` 13-bit, offset
      struct benar.
   2. expr.rs `Expr::MemberAccess` rvalue: chain PENUH (collect_member_chain
      + resolve_struct_chain) → `IrExpr::RangeSelect(sid, msb, lsb)` —
      konsisten dgn lvalue. Konvensi argumen `(msb, lsb)` (sempat terbalik
      → width mismatch WR0102 rhs=1 palsu).
   Test: test_nested_struct_member_access — lvalue+rvalue struct bersarang,
   verifikasi NILAI aktual (r_wip=1, r_err=4'hA width 4) + lebar signal.

BUG 2 — Package dedup first-wins vs module dedup last-wins:

   englishbreakfast) dengan item BERBEDA; module di-dedup "tie → definisi

   PERTAMA (darjeeling) sedangkan module = varian TERAKHIR → struct field
   yang hanya ada di varian terakhir (`io_div4_measure_err.d`) tidak
   ter-resolve (E2001). Fix: `pkg_items.insert(k, v)` (last-wins) konsisten
   dgn pilihan module. Test: test_duplicate_package_last_wins — dua definisi

═══ ROUND 52 — SIM-11: X Propagation Mode (test + BUG FIX) ═══

SIM-11 (No X propagation mode, Critical) — ternyata SUDAH diimplementasi
(XPropagationMode Optimistic/Pessimistic/XAnywhere di types.rs; value.rs
set/get_xprop_mode; CLI --xprop main.rs:1582; maria-env xprop.rs) TAPI
tidak ada test langsung — dan test pertama menangkap BUG NYATA:
   - BUG (ditemukan test): pessimistic BitAnd `0 & X` dan BitOr `1 | X`
     mengembalikan nilai deterministik (0/1) — SAMA dgn optimistic —
     karena urutan match `(Zero,_) => Zero` / `(One,_) => One` SEBELUM
     `_ => X` di bitwise_op. Mode pessimistic tak pernah berbeda untuk
     BitAnd/BitOr (pessimistic didefinisikan "any X → X").
   - FIX (value.rs): cek X/Z PALING PERTAMA di cabang pessimistic
     BitAnd (`(X,_)|(_,X)|(Z,_)|(_,Z) => X`, lalu One&One=One, else Zero)
     dan BitOr (`X/Z dulu => X`, lalu One menang, else Zero).
   - Test: 5 unit baru value.rs — test_xprop_optimistic_bitand_masks_x
     (0 & X = 0), test_xprop_pessimistic_bitand_propagates_x (0 & X = X;
     SEBELUM fix FAIL), test_xprop_optimistic_bitor_masks_x (1 | X = 1),
     test_xprop_pessimistic_bitor_propagates_x (1 | X = X; sebelum fix
     FAIL), test_xprop_pessimistic_eq_x_returns_x (5 == X = X). Mode
     di-restore (thread-local) agar test lain tak terpengaruh.
   - Verifikasi batch (daftar lama `[]` — sudah ada): SIM-11 XPropagation
     (di-atas), SIM-16 force/release (forced_signals + 6 test), ELAB-11
     MICD elaborate persistence (store/restore_elaborate_ir + 2 test),
     VERIF-38 solve-before (before_map + test_randomize_dist_if_solve_) →
     semua di-tandai ✅.

     scheduling di-nonaktifkan → probe11=1 (b berubah seketika) → FAIL.

═══ ROUND 58 — VERIF-30: coverpoint bins (ignore/illegal/normal) + BUG FIX ═══

VERIF-30 (No coverpoint ignore_bins/illegal_bins beyond basic, Medium):
bin eksplisit di-parse parser tapi di-drop elaborator (IrCoverpoint tak punya
bins) dan diabaikan sampler (hanya auto-binning default).

Implementasi:
   - maria-ast types.rs — BinDef.range_list: Vec<Expr> → Vec<BinRange>
     {low, high: Option<Expr>}: `{[1:5]}` (range) tidak ambigu dgn `{1, 5}`
     (dua nilai) — sebelumnya dua expr didorong tanpa penanda range.
   - maria-ir ir.rs — IrBin {name, ranges, bin_type} + IrCoverpoint.bins.
   - elaborator ext.rs — elaborate_covergroups menurunkan bins.
   - simulator coverage.rs sample_covergroup — ignore_bins: sampel
     dikecualikan (cover_total TIDAK naik); illegal_bins: laporan
     AssertionFailed "coverage illegal_bins hit" + tidak dihitung;
     bins normal: hit dicatat ke bin eksplisit (bukan auto-bin); sisanya
     auto-bin default seperti sebelumnya.

⚠️ BUG PARSER LAMA (ditemukan test): range-list `{...}` ditutup `}` yang
TIDAK dikonsumsi — skip_semi tidak melihat `;`, bins loop break di `}` →
hanya bin PERTAMA coverpoint yang ter-parse, bin ke-2+ di-skip diam-diam.
Fix: konsumsi `}` di akhir range-list (stmt.rs).

Test (+3 e2e, semua diverifikasi menangkap bug — matikan matching → FAIL):
   - test_covergroup_ignore_bins_excluded: ignore {[5:7]} + keep {[0:4]} →
     a=6 dikecualikan (total=1), a=3 → bin keep terisi, tanpa fix total=2.
   - test_covergroup_illegal_bins_error: illegal {[100:200]} → a=150 lapor
     illegal_bins hit + hits=1 (bukan 2); tanpa fix tidak ada diagnostic.
   - test_covergroup_explicit_bin_list_values: bins two = {2, 4} → dua
     nilai kena bin two, nilai 3 di luar → auto-bin.

✅ LANG-03 PSL (PROPERTY SPECIFICATION LANGUAGE) — BOOLEAN (ROUND 74):
   - Latar: item [] "Tidak ada PSL (IEEE 1850)" — `assert always (...)` /
     `assert never (...)` tidak dikenal parser (assert diikuti keyword
     Always → parse_immediate_assertion error → assertion di-skip penuh).
   - Parser (stmt.rs parse_immediate_assertion): branch PSL — setelah
     assert, jika token berikutnya `always` (Token::Always) atau Ident
     "never" → parse `(expr)` + optional `@(posedge/negedge/edge clk)` +
     `;` → Stmt::Assert ber-clock. `always` = properti harus true tiap
     cycle; `never` = properti tidak boleh true (body dibalik dengan
     Expr::UnaryOp Not sebelum ternary). Jalur PropertyAssert (ROUND 71)
     mengubahnya jadi always block ber-clock → dievaluasi tiap posedge,
     pass/fail dicatat di assertion_stats (VERIF-27).
   - Directive `default clock = posedge clk;` — arm Token::Default di
     module body (lib.rs): parse & buang sampai ';' (tidak ada efek
     runtime — klaim PSL kita menyebut clock eksplisit).
   - Operator temporal PSL (`|->`, `until`, `before`, `next`, `[*]` dll)
     tidak dikenal lexer → parse_expr gagal → rollback (module-level arm
     Assert/Assume/Cover/Restrict) + skip assertion — modul tetap utuh,
     perilaku sama temporal SVA LANG-04. ⚠️ PSL temporal/full tetap open.
   - Test (2): test_psl_boolean_assert_always_never (assert always
     cnt<=8 + assert never cnt>8 @posedge — pass ≥8, fail 0),
     test_psl_temporal_skipped_safe (|-> + until di-skip, modul utuh).📊 VERIFIKASI ROUND 74:

📊 VERIFIKASI ROUND 76 — LANG-04 overlap implication `|->`:
   - cargo build --workspace: 0 error
   - cargo test --workspace: 2064 passed, 0 failed
     — baseline ROUND 75: 2063; +1 test LANG-04 overlap implication
       (vacuous `req=0 |-> ack` → 0 fail); 0 regresi.
   - Fix: `|->` overlap implication (IEEE §1800-2017 §16.9.2) penuh:
     Lexer: `Token::PipeArrow` (`|->`). AST: `Sequence::Implication`.
     IR: `IrSequence::Implication(ante, cons)`. Elaborator:
     `Sequence::Implication` → `IrSequence::Implication`. Evaluator:
     `ante_matched` field di `SequenceAttempt` — antecedent hanya di-check
     pada `elapsed=0` (posedge pembuatan), consequent dievaluasi dari
     cycle yang sama. Vacuous truth: ante tidak pernah match → pass.

   - Audit item: "FEAT-06:[] Tcl scripting interface — High" → ✅ SELESAI.
   - Implementasi (3 file baru di crates/mivon-tools/src/tcl/ + wiring CLI):
     * mod.rs — interpreter Tcl 8.6 subset: substitusi $var/${var}/[cmd],
       escape backslash, word braced {..} (tanpa substitusi) & quoted "..";
       perintah set/puts/incr/append/lappend/unset/expr/if/while/for/
